media: enable exit-time destructor checks in cdm, mojo, and formats

Enable -Wexit-time-destructors across ClearKey CDM, media/mojo unit
tests, and media/formats unit tests. Move decoder config test
constants into MojoGpuVideoAcceleratorFactoriesTest, inline
parameterized test structs into testing::Values, and initialize
ClearKey CDM's non-component base::AtExitManager process-lifetime
service singleton inside INITIALIZE_CDM_MODULE with NoDestructor.

CONV=24fb0bac-49f1-4a9f-9459-e507cfd2dbfd

44cee7ee19b0e7f04d626bc1f74bd6a5f35e25ee [ diff ] 
ui: enable exit-time destructors check in ui test targets

Eliminate static objects with non-trivial destructors in Skia Gold
pixel diff test support, events_unittests, and gfx_unittests by
using trivially destructible pointers for global pixel diff state,
moving fixture defaults and mock font fields onto test instances,
and making static lookup and parameterized test tables constexpr.
Enable the exit-time destructors warning on these three targets.

TAG=agy
CONV=24fb0bac-49f1-4a9f-9459-e507cfd2dbfd

Reland "Reland "ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions""

This is a reland of commit ff9c8d4945e0d6baab644fa395cf812e9c2ce44d.
Diff is in https://crrev.com/c/8687765/1..2; it also disables the
paint stability timeout for ActorSkipObservationDelayTest.

Verified with 100 runs on the previously failing trybot (using UTR).

Original change's description:
> Reland "ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions"
>
> This is a reland of commit 51de1aad9a337fd7a7e45bac037a5338baec4ab2.
> Diff is in https://crrev.com/c/8669854/1..2.
>
> Original change's description:
> > ttc: Skip the actor observation delay for TTC actions
> >
> > TTC doesn't take an observation after performing an action; page context
> > is delivered separately by TtcPageContextMonitor. Waiting for the page
> > to settle (ObservationDelayController) therefore only delays the tool
> > response to the model, by up to 10s on busy pages.
> >
> > Add ToolRequest::SetSkipObservationDelay(), which makes
> > actor::ToolController complete a tool as soon as it finishes invoking
> > without creating an ObservationDelayController. Follow-up actions
> > enqueued by a tool (e.g. OpenUrlTool's NavigateTool) inherit the flag
> > from the action that enqueued them. TTC sets it on every action it
> > performs.
> >
> > Bug: 571644190
> > Change-Id: I811fd7c91cf04ec183caf5db07902a9218811729
> > Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8659320
> > Reviewed-by: David Bokan <bokan@chromium.org>
> > Commit-Queue: Adithya Srinivasan <adithyas@chromium.org>
> > Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1729054}
>
> Bug: 571644190
> Change-Id: I8471127629def8a22f1be0545810552cf8b703de
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8669854
> Commit-Queue: Adithya Srinivasan <adithyas@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: David Bokan <bokan@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1730334}

Bug: 571644190

Shrink ListedElement by 16 bytes

Narrow ListedElement's two enums to uint8_t and declare the one-byte
fields before the Member and String fields, so they fill the padding
after the vtable pointer.

sizeof(ListedElement):
  64-bit, compressed Member (x64/arm64): 48 -> 32 bytes
  64-bit, uncompressed Member:           56 -> 48 bytes
  32-bit (arm/x86):                      36 -> 24 bytes

Form controls, HTMLObjectElement and form-associated ElementInternals
shrink by the same amount. Add ASSERT_SIZE to keep it from regressing.

No behavior change.

content: enable exit-time destructor checks in shell and web_test

Eliminate exit-time destructors in content_shell_lib and
web_test_renderer so both targets compile with
-Wexit-time-destructors enabled. Hold Shell's quit closure,
shell-created callback and window list in raw pointers scoped to the
Shell lifecycle, cache isolated context origins on
ShellContentBrowserClient, move ContentShellWindowDelegate's
accessibility handle into its Objective-C++ instance variables, and
compute web test directory paths on demand from PathService.

tree
046505aa13300b745b26376238209d9e3681509a
parent
c309c767bb35b9b3e83cd99dbb0f22e256b8b654 [ diff ] 
BidirectionalStream: Don't save alt-service information with bad cert.

When the user allows an invalid cert for a BidirectionalStream, and the
connection tries to provide alt-service information, ignore the alt
service information. This matches behavior elsewhere (e.g., in
HttpNetworkTransaction::ProcessAltSvcHeader()).

The reason for this is that this information is persisted, so a single
server allowed to use a bad cert once, could create an alt service
entry that would persist for years. While alt-service entries do not
bypass cert validity checks, we'd still try to talk to the alt service
location every time a user connects to a server, which would not be
great.

glic: enable exit-time destructor checks in test targets

Replace file-scope and function-local objects that register exit-time
destructors in glic test and test-support targets with constexpr data,
test-scoped locals, and instance-owned configuration, and remove
unused DeepQuery globals so -Wexit-time-destructors can be enabled.

checkout_glic_e2e_tests is true until internal/constants.h drops its
std::vector<std::string> global.

71cad5e3636a374b4e57c1cd089de0ba959bbb41
parent
3e1857e3189b49acfbd8d99b9db0c3bd6e261c12 [ diff ] 
Revert CHECK to DCHECK in WebAppShimManagerDelegate::LaunchApp

In release builds, AppIsInstalled(profile, app_id) can evaluate to false
when an app shim is reopened or launched for an app that is no longer
installed for the given profile, causing a crash when checked via a
CHECK macro.

This change reverts the CHECK back to a DCHECK with a CHECK-exclusion
comment until we are confident it will not be triggered in release.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/18921d1b08400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

📋 Metadata

274ab32a5370f05197b775161aad5420a4b55e3d

author

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 31 04:48:36 2026
tree
25d9a1c5a74b83feaafcd93f80919f109268209f
parent
54ddc0f4804ad5382a3964b77d3501fc5586a0cb [ diff ] 
[Ash] Use Time::NextLocalMidnight() and LocalMidnightWithOffset()

Replace `local_midnight + base::Days(1)` and `base::Days(2)` in
`disabled_auth_message_view.cc`, `quick_insert_preview_metadata.cc`, and
`justifications.cc` with `base::Time::NextLocalMidnight()` and
`LocalMidnightWithOffset(2)`.

This fixes a DST bug: adding `base::Days(1)` or `base::Days(2)`
(multiples of 24 hours) to `LocalMidnight()` lands at 23:00 of the
previous day across a 25-hour DST fall-back day and at 01:00 across a
23-hour spring-forward day, misclassifying timestamps in the 1-hour
window around midnight.

gpu: enable exit-time destructors check in cc, vulkan, and gl tests

Eliminate static objects with non-trivial destructors across cc
perftests, vulkan test support, and gl unit tests by moving
TaskEnvironment ownership onto CCTestSuite, managing native test
window state through trivially destructible pointers cleaned up on
window teardown, and storing fake GL extension strings on the
GLApiTest fixture. Enable the exit-time destructors warning on those
targets while keeping the suppression on cc_unittests.

parent
4f59cbeaad8e355998ac7656bb18cddcaa984e24 [ diff ] 
[WebUI Toolbar] Focus the WebUI omnibox on drop

Like OmniboxViewViews::PerformDrop(), WebUIReadOnlyOmnibox now focuses
the omnibox when text, a URL or a file is dropped into it, before
selecting the dropped text. The focus is not user initiated, so it
neither unelides the text nor opens the popup.

As with the native omnibox, focusing an inactive browser window asks for
it to be activated (see FocusManager::SetFocusedViewWithReason()).
Whether that happens is up to the platform. For example, on Wayland the
compositor refuses it for a drag from another application, which keeps
that application in the foreground. This matches the native omnibox.

Bug: 572001190

3904780230c20a0e505f95e8108da01fdf9c2085

Sat Oct 31 03:29:01 2026
tree
a491f8ac27bb20a7f7a3098c7d2f3c4c63cf6728
parent
08cd912acccaf5bf14cbd26767ce9352359b58e7 [ diff ] 
Extensions: Use JniRawPtr in NativeMessagePort

Replace the raw long mNativePtr field in NativeMessageAndroidPort with
JniRawPtr so JNI Zero tracks the borrowed reference to
::extensions::NativeMessageAndroidPort with BackupRefPtr protection
across JNI calls.

Pass jni_zero::MakeRaw(this) when constructing the Java peer and invoke
JniRawPtr.release() inside destroy() when the C++ owner detaches so
stale native invocations fail safely.

[Android] Allow dropping an image dragged from this app
📋 Metadata
commit
072df1d3f0790a43989889df2fe86ac98a7da54d
[ log ]
author
Joel Hockey <joelhockey@chromium.org>
Sat Oct 31 03:27:24 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 31 03:27:24 2026
tree
eb2151b9ca339237d280e3e2d228b726471ae94e
parent
2b0cfb77d79f37f5c1bac2498eafc9855f633284 [ diff ] 
[Android] Allow dropping an image dragged from this app

Since the ClipboardConfusedDeputyDefenseFiles defense, EventForwarder
rejects dropped content URIs served by this app. Dragging an image puts
a URI of this app's DropDataContentProvider on the drag, so an image
dragged from one tab could no longer be dropped into another.

A new EventForwarder.isUriSafeForDrop(), similar to
ClipboardImpl.isUriSafeForRead(), now allows URIs of this app's
DropDataProvider, only when they resolve to a non-exported provider of
this app, and still rejects any other content URI of this app.
DropDataProvider only serves the image that this app is dragging, and
only until shortly after the drag ends.

Both Uri.getAuthority() and the authority of the string form must be
DropDataProvider's, since on Android Q a Uri from another app's parcel
can have a getAuthority() which differs from its string form, and the
string form is what is later opened.

WebView: Destroy popup native if receiver is gone
📋 Metadata
commit
2b0cfb77d79f37f5c1bac2498eafc9855f633284
[ log ]
author
Keigo Oka <oka@google.com>
Sat Oct 31 03:26:29 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Oct 31 03:26:29 2026
tree
1d17f5e043270709304961a62a60ae28b4a2751b
parent
73d0cb338e530bf0686db4ab2e9b3c3a12fc9a28 [ diff ] 
WebView: Destroy popup native if receiver is gone

When the AwContents that should host a popup has already been
destroyed, receivePopupContents() returned without deleting the native
AwContents it was handed. The opener gave up ownership in
releasePopupAwContents(), so nothing else deletes it, and the native
AwContents leaks together with its WebContents.

Delete it in that case, as supplyContentsForPopup() already does when
the app supplies no AwContents for the popup. The new PopupWindowTest
case fails without this change: the native instance count stays one
higher than expected.

This is also needed before AwContents can be owned through
JniUniquePtr, whose LifetimeAssert fails when a JniUniquePtr is
garbage collected without destroy().

0fa6b0bc10edc2c168a101e3fd294309e63c10d0
parent
e004a2a2b10f4dafcf027329fb1b6180c16b7c53 [ diff ] 
Keep QUIC server network stats when a session attempt is cancelled

QuicSessionPool::ProcessGoingAwaySession() clears the server's
ServerNetworkStats (SRTT and bandwidth estimate) when a session closes
before its handshake is confirmed. A session closed because its attempt
was cancelled takes the same path, although the cancellation says
nothing about the path to the server.

AsyncDnsJob races connection attempts and cancels the loser as soon as
another attempt wins, so every such race clears the stats recorded by
an earlier session. The winner records new stats only when it closes.
Until then, GetTimeDelayForWaitingJob() falls back to the 300ms default
and new handshakes to the server lose the cached initial RTT.

Skip the clear when the session was closed locally with
QUIC_CONNECTION_CANCELLED. quic::QuicSession::error() cannot be used for
this check because QuicChromiumClientSession::OnConnectionClosed()
notifies the pool before the base class records the close frame, so the
session now records the close error next to the close source.

Bug: 531975349

473caf0d686086d01b446319f7278f026708f57c [ diff ] 
Remove simdutf implementation of base::Base64Encode()

Remove the simdutf implementation of base::Base64Encode() and associated
code. This implementation was never used outside of local experiments
and benchmarks because it potentially violates the security rule of 2.
While it can be faster than the Rust implementation since it supports
AVX512, the memory safety concern makes it a non-viable approach.

This CL is AI generated.

[PA] Skip ThreadCache poisoning for zapped SchedulerLoopQuarantine slots

`ThreadCache::PutInBucket()` poisons the remainder of the first 64-byte
cache line with `0xbadbad00` (`HAS_FREELIST_SHADOW_ENTRY` on x86_64)
before writing the freelist entry. In `perf annotate` of
`SchedulerLoopQuarantinePerfTest` (`thinlto_optimize_max`), this
poisoning loop accounts for 32.4% of CPU samples in
`SchedulerLoopQuarantineBranch::PurgeInternal()`. When
`enable_zapping_` is true (as in `browser_main` and
`browser_main_unlimited`), `Quarantine()` already zaps the entire
usable slot with `kFreedByte` (`0xcd`) upon entry to the quarantine,
making the second poisoning pass in `PutInBucket()` redundant.

This CL templates `ThreadCache::MaybePutInCache()` and
`ThreadCache::PutInBucket()` on `bool poison = true`, and calls
`MaybePutInCache<false>()` from `ReleaseDequarantinedSlot()` when
`enable_zapping_` is true.

SchedulerLoopQuarantinePerfTest results on Pinpoint ("all lines" rows
read every cache line of each object before freeing it, like a
destructor that reads all fields):

Significance: ◆ p < 0.05, ◆◆ p < 0.01, ◆◆◆ p < 0.001

Windows (win-11-perf, 30 runs), config: browser_main - https://pinpoint-dot-chromeperf.appspot.com/job/11aa2d3b190000

- Positive impact: On Windows (x86_64), reduces time by 3.8%-6.3% across
  all `storm_*` benchmarks and by 7.6%-8.0% on `steady_churn`
  (`browser_main`).
- No impact: On Windows `steady_churn` (`unlimited`), `RunSteadyChurn()`
  stops its timer before the end-of-task purge, so no slots are
  dequarantined during the timed window. On Mac (ARM64),
  `PutInBucket()`'s poisoning loop is guarded by
  `PA_BUILDFLAG(PA_ARCH_CPU_X86_64)`, so codegen is unchanged.
- Negative impact: None.

[MemoryCoordinator] Don't release disk-writing consumers on critical pressure
📋 Metadata
commit
a17c5862dfc7dd08b4b945245f7c9727dc49e54a
[ log ]
author
Patrick Monette <pmonette@chromium.org>
Sat Oct 31 02:00:22 2026
committer

Sat Oct 31 02:00:22 2026
tree
d6494c35da409d63b14ffa5284cc195591de7fa0
parent
445cb045e270111ab38ba7beb2964c4ac4efce72 [ diff ] 
[MemoryCoordinator] Don't release disk-writing consumers on critical pressure

Under critical memory pressure, the system is probably already swapping
out memory and making heavy use of disk IO. Consumers that release
memory by writing it to disk (ReleaseMemoryDataAccess::
kReadsAndWritesToDisk) would page that memory back in only to write it
out again, compounding the problem. BlobMemoryController, the only such
consumer, has been ignoring critical pressure on its own for that
reason.

Move that decision to MemoryPressureListenerPolicy: on critical
pressure, these consumers still get the lowered limit, so they stop
growing, but are not asked to release memory. BlobMemoryController's own
critical pressure check is now redundant and is removed.

No behavior change by default: BlobMemoryController only acts on its
limit when asked to release memory, which no longer happens under
critical pressure. Its stateful mode, which reacts to limit changes, is
disabled behind kBlobStatefulMemoryPressure and ignores limits below the
moderate pressure level.

b6824f1a37e04b40b42a9a0807e068783b4afdc8 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Add Android.Omnibox.SuggestionList.KeystrokeToUiModel.{First,Last}

The existing
Android.Omnibox.SuggestionList.RequestToUiModel.{First,Last} histograms
start their clock when the autocomplete request is issued, which happens
inside the deferred action scheduled by
AutocompleteMediator.onTextChanged - i.e. after the 30ms
OMNIBOX_SUGGESTION_START_DELAY_MS has already elapsed. They therefore
exclude the deferral between the keystroke and the request start, and
cannot be used to evaluate changes to that deferral (crbug/578525544).
Android currently has no keystroke-anchored suggestion latency metric.

This CL adds KeystrokeToUiModel.{First,Last}, which measure from the
keystroke (captured synchronously in onTextChanged) to the same end
points as RequestToUiModel, with the same bucketing and cancel semantics
so the two are directly comparable: a refining keystroke or ending the
interaction drops the pending .Last. The new histograms are only
recorded for typed-text requests, not zero-prefix requests.
RequestToUiModel is left unchanged to preserve longitudinal
comparability.

b78052358489f3d745f3c922a012a1d52d9f75ea
parent
e28940e01e2826de58d938c6b097b57c495dca92 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Lazily create ActionChipsView; reuse chip adapters

Previously, every BaseSuggestionView eagerly constructed and attached an
ActionChipsView (a RecyclerView + LinearLayoutManager) in its
constructor, even though most suggestions do not have action chips.
Additionally, BaseSuggestionViewBinder called ActionChipsBinder.bind()
for every suggestion property key, and ActionChipsBinder allocated a new
SimpleRecyclerViewAdapter on every bind of ACTION_CHIPS even when the
bound ModelList had not changed.

This change:
1. Defers ActionChipsView instantiation in BaseSuggestionView to
   ensureActionChipsView(), only creating and attaching it when
   BaseSuggestionViewProperties.ACTION_CHIPS is non-null. ACTION_CHIPS
   moves from ActionChipsProperties to the containing view's
   properties and ActionChipsProperties is removed.
2. Binds ACTION_CHIPS and DEVICE_FORM_FACTOR directly in
   BaseSuggestionViewBinder (ActionChipsBinder is removed), skipping
   the carousel entirely when ACTION_CHIPS is null and no
   ActionChipsView exists. BaseSuggestionView caches lead-in spacing
   and fading-edge settings so a lazily created carousel picks them up.
3. Has BaseSuggestionViewBinder manage the carousel adapter: the attached
   adapter is reused when it already presents the suggestion's chip
   list, swapped (via swapAdapter, so the RecycledViewPool survives)
   when the list changes, and detached when chips are cleared.
   ActionChipsView holds no model state and never sees the ModelList.
4. Makes chip theming a property of the chip model. ActionChipProperties
   (which also hosts the carousel ViewType) extends ChipProperties with
   RESOURCE_PROVIDER; ActionChipsProcessor
   sets it, and ActionChipViewBinder applies the cached scheme-dependent
   background tint on bind. This replaces the incognito theme overlay
   (whose only effective difference was the chip background color) and
   lets recycled ChipViews be themed on rebind instead of re-inflated.
5. Avoids redundant SpacingRecyclerViewItemDecoration replacement in
   ActionChipsView.setLeadInSpacing() when spacing is unchanged.

[Fusebox] Transcode images Blink cannot decode (HEIF, DNG) before upload
📋 Metadata
commit
e28940e01e2826de58d938c6b097b57c495dca92
[ log ]
author

Sat Oct 31 01:51:43 2026
committer

Sat Oct 31 01:51:43 2026
tree
7c449579d25fcc6dc49338ad8801b07bdc978316
parent
3792f15ee47c64e28a183a530e559e541fca91fa [ diff ] 
[Fusebox] Transcode images Blink cannot decode (HEIF, DNG) before upload

Gallery uploads of cloud-backed ("needs to be prepared") photos failed
with VALIDATION_FAILED / IMAGE_PROCESSING_ERROR. Histograms showed the
Java fetcher handing native a complete payload (AttachmentReadResult =
SUCCESS) while Blink returned a null bitmap for every file
(Lens.Composebox.ImageUpload.File.InputImageInvalid = true). A failing
file was confirmed to be HEIC. Blink's image decoders select by
signature and support JPEG, PNG, GIF, WebP, BMP, ICO and AVIF only;
HEIF/HEIC and TIFF-based DNG are not decodable, whereas Android's
ImageDecoder handles both. This is also why enabling AIM image
downscaling (which re-encodes through ImageDecoder) made uploads work.

This change adds ImageFormatSniffer, which identifies an attachment's
container from its leading bytes (the declared mime type is not
trustworthy: the picker reported image/jpeg for the HEIC payload) and
answers whether Blink can decode it. FuseboxAttachmentDetailsFetcher
now transcodes to JPEG via ImageDecoder, independent of the downscaling
flag, whenever:

* the bytes sniff as a format Blink cannot decode (HEIF, TIFF/DNG,
  JPEG XL), regardless of declared mime type; or
* the signature is unrecognised but the provider declared an image, in
  which case Blink would fail anyway so the platform decoder gets a
  chance first.

When transcoding succeeds, the attachment mime type is rewritten to
image/jpeg. If the platform decoder cannot decode the bytes (or hits
OOM), declared images fail locally (DECODE_FAILED) instead of
forwarding bytes native is guaranteed to reject, while undeclared
attachments keep their raw bytes and upload as plain files. The C2PA
raw-bytes bypass is not applied in these cases. The downscaling path
now also keys its output format off the sniffed format rather than the
declared mime type.

Adds Omnibox.MobileFusebox.AttachmentImageFormat (sniffed format) and
Omnibox.MobileFusebox.AttachmentImageFormatMismatch (declared mime type
disagrees with the bytes).

Follow-up to crrev.com/c/8625769, which added the read hardening and the
AttachmentReadResult histogram used to diagnose this.

Fixed: 576042051

410490f18f225edf97d9313309fcc6af0d7a2d86
parent
b0e196e4eff0d2c6630bff725177c603491ee5a1 [ diff ] 
gpu: Optimize CollectContextGraphicsInfo for kGpuInitOptimization

On Windows, when ANGLE runs on D3D11 (not WARP), kGpuInitOptimization
is enabled and ANGLE's ID3D11Device is available, collect context
graphics info directly from the D3D11 device in the new
CollectD3D11GraphicsInfo() and skip CollectGraphicsInfoGL():
- d3d11_feature_level comes from ID3D11Device::GetFeatureLevel().
- vertex/pixel_shader_version are derived from the feature level,
  mirroring ANGLE's Renderer11::getMajor/MinorShaderModel(), instead of
  parsing GL_RENDERER. GPU.D3DShaderModel is recorded from it as well.
- The active GPU is identified via IdentifyActiveGPUWithLuid(), falling
  back to the primary GPU.
- display_type, gl_vendor (ANGLE's GL_VENDOR equals its EGL_VENDOR),
  gl_ws_vendor/version/extensions and gl_reset_notification_strategy
  come from the EGL display via the new
  CollectGraphicsInfoFromANGLEDisplay(), without a current context.
  It's platform independent so other ANGLE platforms can reuse it.
- gl_renderer and gl_version also come from the EGL display, through
  ANGLE's new EGL_ANGLE_query_renderer_strings extension. gl_version is
  built from the ANGLE version and the default GLContextAttribs ES
  version, which is what the context in CollectGraphicsInfoGL() uses.
  Chromium's EGL bindings support for the extension is added in the
  parent CL.

CollectDriverInfoD3D() now also sets driver_vendor for every adapter,
derived from the PCI vendor id like ANGLE's GL_RENDERER does (or
ANGLE's hex format for unknown vendors). It already set driver_version
from the adapter's UMD version, so both are known without GL strings.

gl_extensions and max_msaa_samples remain empty in this path.

All other configurations (WARP, SwiftShader, other ANGLE backends, or
the feature disabled) keep going through CollectGraphicsInfoGL(). In
that path, GPU.D3DShaderModel is now recorded from the D3D11 feature
level.

tree
1f4a066b6b9803d1f13cfcaac77dc8808b8b5202
parent
26b018c38497e63477a0ae20c3bd711c00f001e6 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Skip verification for built-in component extensions

Built-in component extensions (mojom::ManifestLocation::kComponent) do
not ship content verification hashes and are not managed by
ExternalProviderManager. Return Mode::NONE for kComponent in
ChromeContentVerifierDelegate::GetVerifyInfo().

Previously, GetVerifyInfo() enabled verification and repair for
kComponent extensions (via MustRemainEnabled()). Skipping kComponent
avoids at login on ChromeOS (~9.2 MiB idle PSS savings):
- Initializing the default StoragePartition and NetworkContext to
  fetch signed hashes for kComponent extensions with a webstore
  update_url.
- Triggering CorruptedExtensionReinstaller on missing hashes, which
  spawns DataDecoderService via ExtensionDownloader to parse update
  manifest XML.

[Reading mode PDFs] Record Accessibility.PdfHeuristics.ListStartReason

Record whether a heuristic list was started by an unordered marker, a
nested-only marker, or an ordered marker starting at or above 1.

This shows which rules start lists in real PDFs, such as how often a
list starts above 1 or only from a nested marker. Those rules carry more
false-positive risk, so the data guides which ones to tune or remove.

[MiraclePtr] Enforce raw_ptr in containers under chrome/browser/ttc

Replace raw pointers (`content::WebContents*`) with
`raw_ptr<content::WebContents>` in `RecordingObserver::changes_` and
`TakeChanges()` across `voice_focused_contents_tracker` tests.

This advances MiraclePtr's expansion to containers and preempts an
upcoming clang-plugin update that will strictly ban these un-rewritten
instances.

tree
f1a4020960a1d9ab2b068d8dab1ad260f97c6007
parent
58cdf601764b815a4af3886566b9fd951c5b2188 [ diff ] 
Attribute zero-state opens to correct pin buttons

When opening the contextual tasks side panel in zero state (for a new
thread) from a pinned button, Show() cannot infer the entry source.
Previously, std::nullopt was passed, causing the entry source to fall
back to kOther.

This explicitly passes kPermanentToolbarButton as the entry source so
metrics for session duration and panel closes are correctly recorded
alongside continue-thread opens.

Bug: b:580017132

tree
2ec8fc548ad92e852ce9b6b77a51959252400c59
parent
3f4971126ff7cf0236032b6c6f52cf37a4487683 [ diff ] 
[Reading mode PDFs] Remap annotations when splitting text runs

Links, highlights, form graphics, and images refer to text runs by
index. Splitting a run shifts the index of every later run, so without
remapping, these annotations would attach to the wrong text.

`pdf::SplitTextRunsAtVerticalDividers()` now also returns the index of
each original run's first part. `pdf::RemapTextRunIndices()` uses it to
remap link, highlight, form graphic, and image text run indices. The
`PdfAccessibilityTreeBuilder` constructor copies and remaps the
annotations only when a run is split.

Bug: 574033561

48d5cf5f6521b865e3b1aa84db150a44d79a0d6a [ diff ] 
Reland "[Tab Declutter] Batch ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest"

This is a reland of commit 27776360a1c74550bb6311522798d66b50583b04

The original CL failed on android-11-x86-fyi-rel (API 30) because
FreshCtaTransitTestRule cleans up between tests via
MultiInstanceManager#closeWindows, which is only implemented in
MultiInstanceManagerApi31 (API 31+) and is a no-op in
MultiInstanceManagerImpl on Android 11. As a result, regular tabs were
persisted and restored across batched tests on Android 11:
- Direct tab count assertions failed (e.g. testRestoreAllInactiveTabs).
- Once 3+ regular tabs accumulated (2+ grid rows), entering the regular
  Tab Switcher scrolled to the active tab on row 2+, pushing the
  ArchiveMessageCard header at position 0 under the toolbar (scale 0.0).

Fix by explicitly force-closing all tabs in mRegularTabModel in
tearDown() (with allowUndo(false) and saveToTabRestoreService(false)),
and synchronously finishing any non-ChromeTabbedActivity (such as
SettingsActivity) so ApplicationStatus#isEveryActivityDestroyed triggers
ArchivedTabModelOrchestrator#destroyProfileKeyedMap between tests.

Original change's description:
> [Tab Declutter] Batch ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest
>
> Part of an effort to speed up chrome_public_test_apk.
>
> ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest (37 tests) was annotated with
> @DoNotBatch even though it already uses FreshCtaTransitTestRule, which
> supports @Batch(Batch.PER_CLASS) by closing all activities and clearing
> instance/tab state between tests while reusing the browser process.
>
> Convert ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest to @Batch(Batch.PER_CLASS) and
> tear down UserActionTester in @After so user action callbacks do not
> accumulate across batched tests.
>
> Locally on an Android 15 x64 emulator, this reduces
> ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest runtime from 304s to 150s (-154s /
> -51%, 2.03x faster), with on-device test execution dropping from 278s to
> 123s (-155s / -56%, 2.26x faster).
>
> Bug: 348068134, 578114288
> Test: run_chrome_public_test_apk -f "*ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest*" --enable-leak-checks
> Change-Id: I160155b3fcd557e946863e2ca9221370586ef602
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8681704
> Auto-Submit: James Cook <jamescook@chromium.org>
> Reviewed-by: Brandon Wylie <wylieb@google.com>
> Commit-Queue: James Cook <jamescook@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1731807}

Bug: 348068134, 578114288, 580067196

[e2e] Decouple top and bottom edge state updates from change updates

This CL:
- refactors to have a "unified" EdgeChanged method
- adds helpers to distinguish between top/bottom state changes & updates
- ensures change observer notifications happen only once when needed
- renames unified components
- Adds unit tests to verify top only edge changes notify
  `ChangeObserver`s without triggering bottom only side effects, and
  that combined top & bottom changes update padding and observers only
  once

[Omnibox] Don't open AskG CoBrowse panel without tab sharing

With Omnibox_AskGCoBrowse enabled, "Ask Google about this page" opened
the contextual tasks side panel even when tab sharing was unavailable
(e.g. in incognito), leaving the panel with no tab attached.

Gate the CoBrowse routing on tab sharing eligibility, matching the check
the side panel uses for tab inputs. When ineligible, AskG falls back to
the Lens overlay and the "opens in side panel" suggestion description is
hidden.

Screenshot: http://screencast/cast/NDU4NDY0MzU2MjA0NTQ0MHwzMmM3NDhjYi1hZg

Fixed: b:579976494
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=ChromeAutocompleteProviderClientAskGCoBrowseTest.*

41884f7a07391babc95d06076edbde9f3a80cce9 [ diff ] 
Fix case insensitive defaulting to historical match instead of verbatim

It is generally helpful for the omnibox to ignore case and automatically
take the default historical suggestion which has an established
navigation's casing. However, when a full path is typed the verbatim
input may be preferred instead due to case sensitivity in URL paths.
Host names are insensitive but paths may be sensitive, depending on how
servers process them.

Fixed: b:389330756

a9757fc63f6095de28bf47f7c3108a6a4195a49c [ diff ] 
[ttc] Add TtcSessionAudioRecorder

Adds the recorder that chrome://ttc-internals will use to keep the exact
audio of a TTC session: what was sent to the server and what the server
sent back, each on its own time-aligned stream. Each chunk is stored as
it arrived, up to a byte cap on the recorded audio, and range reads are
clamped to recorded audio. Audio placed after a given time can be
discarded, for server audio that stopped playing early because the user
interrupted it.

Also adds //chrome/browser/ttc/debug, where the debug tools live, with
its OWNERS, and hooks its unit tests into unit_tests. Nothing uses the
recorder yet.

Part 4 of the chrome://ttc-internals series. Design: go/ttc-internals

37cc753cc9c68422ec2feef0bdac30d6015b53eb
parent
f3d967879a012c0e89dbd13c928ec7bf26d7da55 [ diff ] 
[AAudio] Clear pull_fifo_ on Stop() and Flush()

When `kAAudioVariableSizedCallbacks` is enabled, `AAudioOutputStream`
uses a 960-frame (`20ms` at 48kHz) `AudioPullFifo` (`pull_fifo_`) to
adapt 20ms renderer buffers to smaller AAudio hardware bursts.

When a playback stops (e.g., when switching videos on m.youtube.com),
`AudioOutputDispatcherImpl::StopStream()` calls
`AAudioOutputStream::Stop()` and places the physical stream into
`idle_streams_` for reuse. Previously, neither `Stop()` nor `Flush()`
called `pull_fifo_->Clear()`, leaving up to 959 unconsumed frames from
the old playback buffered inside `pull_fifo_`. When the reused physical
stream was started for the next video during `InitializeSink()`, the
first AAudio callbacks drained those stale frames as an audible
pop/burst before silence and the new video's audio began.

Clear `pull_fifo_` under `lock_` in `AAudioOutputStream::Stop()` and
`AAudioOutputStream::Flush()`, and add unit tests verifying that no
stale frames leak across `Stop()` or `Flush()`.

Verification:
- Repro (Before): `AAudioOutputStreamTest.StopClearsPullFifo` and
  `FlushClearsPullFifo` -> 2 failed (stale 0.75f / 0.5f samples replayed
  from `pull_fifo_` without querying new source)
- Fix (After): `AAudioOutputStreamTest.StopClearsPullFifo` and
  `FlushClearsPullFifo` -> 2 passed
- Regression: `gn check out/Android "//media/audio:*"` and
  `AAudioOutputStreamTest.*` -> 2 passed, 0 failures

TAG=agy
CONV=48c5e832-3cab-4a87-8b2b-0a33b6fb8817

[Glic] Thread RenderFrameHost to Android invokeWithPrompt for policy checks

The Android "Ask Gemini" text selection entry point pre-populates the
selected text into Gemini via GlicInvokeOptions::prompts. To evaluate
enterprise DataControlsRules against the source page, GlicInvokeOptions
requires the source frame's GlobalRenderFrameHostId in prompt_policy.

Thread the frame that owns the selection (the focused frame, matching
desktop's RenderViewContextMenu::ExecGlic source_frame) from
TextSelectionActionMenuDelegate through
GlicKeyedServiceHandler.invokeWithPrompt() to
GlicKeyedServiceAndroid::InvokeWithPrompt(), which sets prompt_policy.
Data Controls evaluate that frame's main-frame URL. Without a valid
tab, frame, or non-empty text, it returns false without invoking Glic.

18e873d587ae8f26a1debabbfbc839716adfc7d9 [ diff ] 
Revert "Prevent net/ from requesting invalid hostnames."

This reverts commit 370a8766e850dec28a81f5f0aacc13f1c86eae98.

Reason for revert:
LUCI Bisection has identified this change as the cause of a test failure. See the analysis: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/bisection/test-analysis/b/5706821961515008

Sample build with failed test: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8666480168227937233
Affected test(s):
[://\:chrome_wpt_tests!webtest::external/wpt/webdriver/tests/bidi/browsing_context/navigate#invalid.py](https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/:%2F%2F%5C:chrome_wpt_tests%21webtest::external%2Fwpt%2Fwebdriver%2Ftests%2Fbidi%2Fbrowsing_context%2Fnavigate%23invalid.py?q=VHash%3A0bc243e3f1e70338)

If this is a false positive, please report it at http://b.corp.google.com/createIssue?component=1199205&description=Analysis%3A+https%3A%2F%2Fci.chromium.org%2Fui%2Fp%2Fchromium%2Fbisection%2Ftest-analysis%2Fb%2F5706821961515008&format=PLAIN&priority=P3&title=Wrongly+blamed+https%3A%2F%2Fchromium-review.googlesource.com%2Fc%2Fchromium%2Fsrc%2F%2B%2F8680296&type=BUG

Original change's description:
> Prevent net/ from requesting invalid hostnames.
>
> The URL spec allows invalid hostnames to appear in URLs. When a webpage
> requests such a URL, they're passed to net/. net/ will typically fail
> such requests as the DNS layer, but if the requests end up not going
> over DNS (e.g., in the case of proxies), we can end up actually trying
> to send an over-the-wire request.
>
> HSTS considers, e.g., http://a!b.c.com not to be a child of
> http://c.com, but the cookie code does, which is problematic. While
> sites should be using secure cookies, and not relying on HSTS to protect
> cookies (which has its own lifetimes, max database size, and expiration
> times), sites could even then use this to write cookies. So the current
> logic is not very safe.
>
> This CL fixes the issue by checking for hostname validity in
> URLRequestHttpJob, and failing requests with ERR_INVALID_URL if the host
> is not valid. This does mean that some domain names that once may have
> worked in niche conditions (like proxies that do weird things) will now
> fail, so this change is guarded by an enabled-by-default base::Feature.
>
> It looks to me like the cookie spec says domain matching should fail on
> these domains, so we should additionally change that, but rejecting
> invalid HTTP URLs, instead of sending them to proxies that might not
> know how to deal with URLs with invalid hostnames, is also a good idea,
> and is a much simpler fix.
>
> This CL also updates a couple of tests that now have failing navigations
> as a result of this change.
>

b2e02dae02398c77523ea372c33e148e8ff53337
parent
bbc7faad495945a3dd1d60dc2b41d03ea2e90171 [ diff ] 
[MemoryCoordinator] Skip MemoryConsumer registration where suppressed

The SuppressMemoryListeners experiment showed that reacting to memory
pressure is not beneficial for some listeners on some platforms. Since
then, these listeners were migrated to base::MemoryConsumer, which
silently made the suppression mask a no-op for them.

This CL restores the experiment's behavior by not registering the
following classes as MemoryConsumers on the platforms where they were
suppressed:
- DiscardableSharedMemoryManager: Mac and Windows.
- GpuChannelManager: Mac and Windows.
- SSLClientSessionCache: Mac and Windows.
- PrerenderHostRegistry: Android.

FrameEvictionManager is intentionally not included, as it is now
covered by the kScalableFrameEviction experiment.

Note that not registering also opts these classes out of all memory
coordinator policies (e.g. ZeroLimitPolicy), not just memory pressure.

Tests that exercise the MemoryConsumer path are compiled out on
platforms where the class does not register.

tree
4e779ad01ac8afa8529b453accfea4006f620d0c
parent
7d949122f5a00cb925cf4cd35261bed1cca6c714 [ diff ] 
Initial seccomp-bpf sandbox for GPU process on Android.

Defines a new seccomp-bpf sandbox for Chromium's GPU process on
Android, currently disabled by default, and defines its first policy,
which is to forbid calls to mprotect() or mmap() passing PROT_EXEC.

This policy is incompatible with dynamically loading libraries; the
dynamic library loader calls mprotect with PROT_EXEC on the library's
mapped pages. A warmup function is added which triggers all of the
lazy-loading paths which have been discovered so far in Chrome's GPU
process. More can be added.

The seccomp-bpf sandbox may be manually enabled via a new about:flags
entry. It logs to about:gpu when it is enabled. A feature flag is
added so the sandbox can be Finched.

Tested manually on Pixel 10 with WebGL, WebGPU, and video content.

[EdgeToEdge] Plumb top safe area insets to Blink for top edge-to-edge

When drawing to the top edge (e.g. viewport-fit=cover web pages with top
edge-to-edge enabled), push the top system bar inset from
EdgeToEdgeControllerImpl to InsetObserver via
updateTopInsetForEdgeToEdge() so it is included in the safe area rect
forwarded to DisplayCutoutController and Blink.

In Blink, update Page::UpdateSafeAreaInsetWithBrowserControls() to
dynamically compute safe-area-inset-top based on
scaled_max_safe_area_insets_.top(), browser_controls.TopHeight(), and
browser_controls.TopShownRatio(), matching the existing bottom safe area
inset calculation.

[UI] Migrate c/b/u/w/ (part 2) to `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition`

Spell the type as `ui::mojom::WindowOpenDisposition` instead of the
deprecated `WindowOpenDisposition` alias, and include
"ui/base/mojom/window_open_disposition.mojom-shared.h" instead of
"ui/base/window_open_disposition.h". Remove includes of the old header
that nothing uses.

Headers that only use the type in declarations include the mojom
forward header instead.

This CL covers:
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/searchbox/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/history/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/commerce/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/settings/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/bookmarks/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/extensions_zero_state_promo/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/print_preview/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/interstitials/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/signin/
  chrome/browser/ui/webui/ (files directly in it)

No behavior change.

This CL was generated by running the following from src/, then
`git cl format`:

  migrate_window_open_disposition.py --migrate-type
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/searchbox chrome/browser/ui/webui/history
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/commerce chrome/browser/ui/webui/settings
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/bookmarks
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/extensions_zero_state_promo
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/print_preview
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/interstitials chrome/browser/ui/webui/signin
        ':(glob)chrome/browser/ui/webui/*'
  check_alias_includes.py --enumerators
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/searchbox chrome/browser/ui/webui/history
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/commerce chrome/browser/ui/webui/settings
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/bookmarks
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/extensions_zero_state_promo
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/print_preview
        chrome/browser/ui/webui/interstitials chrome/browser/ui/webui/signin
        ':(glob)chrome/browser/ui/webui/*'

The scripts are attached to https://crbug.com/373551504.

ba917e9fd47789ba982b472701a77d3081f2ac5b [ diff ] 
updater: fix OOP fetcher dial failure and POST status logging

If DialFetchService() failed, OutOfProcessNetworkFetcher ran a
completion callback that had already been moved into the event-logging
wrapper, which crashed. Run the wrapped callback instead, and dial
before creating the download file.

Also pass the wrapped ResponseStartedCallback to the PostRequest
observer, as the other fetch paths do.

Telemetry changes (NetworkEvent):
- An OOP POST with a non-2xx response now reports the HTTP status in
  error_code instead of 0.
- A dial failure is now logged with the dial error instead of
  crashing.

99fdfdbbc56e122afa724727e10a67b2363db04e
parent
dc35ef57095dabed9dc2dbb3e5d6ea625165c84c [ diff ] 
updater: fix DownloadStreamReader race in macOS OOP fetcher

OutOfProcessNetworkFetcher::DownloadToFile gave its DownloadStreamReader
to the OnDownloadComplete callback, which destroyed the reader when it
returned. If OnDownloadComplete arrived before the reader saw the data
pipe close, the completion callback was dropped and the download hung.

The fetcher now owns the reader until the reader reports completion.
The reader tracks pipe EOF with a flag and resets its DataPipeDrainer
through a WeakPtr, so it can be destroyed at any time.

Fixed: 579453946

3800cb9c4c803dc963e57c0d3482103d59f05eb3
parent
6b1d2053b4fe00f04dec0ec4d9b79c10bfa4caac [ diff ] 
[Enterprise] Fix and re-enable `PrintIfAllowedByPolicyWarnedThenBypass`

Re-enable
`PrintContentAnalysisUtilsTest.PrintIfAllowedByPolicyWarnedThenBypass`
in `chrome/browser/enterprise/data_protection/print_utils_unittest.cc`.

This test was disabled in https://crrev.com/c/6786168 due to
multi-platform flakiness under https://crbug.com/434099738.

In `PrintIfAllowedByPolicyWarnedThenBypass`, two reporting events are
uploaded: `EVENT_RESULT_WARNED` (from the initial scanning verdict) and
`EVENT_RESULT_BYPASSED` (from the user bypass). Both events are uploaded
asynchronously via `RealtimeReportingClientBase::UploadSecurityEvent`,
which posts a task to `base::ThreadPool` to fetch local IP addresses
before replying to the UI thread. Because `base::ThreadPool` tasks run
concurrently and unsequenced, the bypass event reply can arrive before
the warn event reply.
`EventReportValidator::ExpectSensitiveDataEventWarnThenBypass`
previously used sequential `.WillOnce(...).WillOnce(...)` `gMock`
expectations, which failed whenever the bypass event arrived first.

This CL resolves the flakiness by refactoring
`EventReportValidator::ExpectSensitiveDataEventWarnThenBypass` to expect
two events via `.Times(2)` using `base::MakeRefCounted` with
`base::RefCountedData` and validating each event against its
corresponding expected proto based on its `event_result()`, executing
`done_closure_` once both events have been verified in any order.

Additionally, while the test was disabled, https://crrev.com/c/8217231
migrated `PrintIfAllowedByPolicyWarnedThenBypass` from dictionary-based
validation to proto-based `DlpSensitiveDataEvent` expectations, omitting
`TriggeredRuleInfo` and `clicked_through` on the bypass proto. This CL
restores `TriggeredRuleInfo` on both expected events and sets
`clicked_through` to `true` on `event_bypass`, replaces `base::RunLoop`
with `base::test::TestFuture` (matching https://crrev.com/c/8190077) so
verdict completion before waiting does not hang, and documents the tests
in `print_utils_unittest.cc`.

Verified on macOS and Linux.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Fixed: 434099738

[FedCM] Re-enable `VisibilityChangesUpdatesDialogPosition`

Re-enable `VisibilityChangesUpdatesDialogPosition` in
`FedCmAccountSelectionViewDesktopTest`.

The test was previously disabled in https://crrev.com/c/7000858 under
https://crbug.com/441908440 with a `TODO` inquiring whether dialog
position update verification remained valid following
`TabDialogManager` integration in https://crrev.com/c/6685146.

In `FedCmAccountSelectionView`, tab foregrounding notifications are
still observed via `tab_->RegisterDidActivate`, which triggers
`TabForegrounded()`. This explicitly calls
`UpdateDialogVisibilityAndPosition()` and updates the dialog position
via `UpdateDialogPosition()` so the dialog adapts if the web contents
was resized while hidden.

Investigation confirms that the test passes cleanly and the failure
could not be reproduced. This CL re-enables the test, removes the
obsolete `TODO`, and updates documentation comments for the test.
Verified on Linux.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[NTP] Keep OneGoogleBar width constant while the LHS rail animates

The OneGoogleBar is a cross-origin iframe sized to #rhsContent, so it is
resized on every frame of the LHS width transition. The stale frame is
drawn anchored at the top-left until the OneGoogleBar renderer catches
up, which leaves a blank strip at the right edge of the screen while the
rail collapses.

Anchor the OneGoogleBar to the inline-end edge with a constant width
when the LHS is present, so it is not re-laid out during the transition.
The surplus width overflows under the LHS, where #rhsContent clips it.
Screencast:
http://screencast/cast/NTA4NTU3NzI0MTE2NTgyNHwwMjY3NWJjYy02OA

[IAU5] Move InAppUpdateController ownership to TabbedRootUiCoordinator

Wiring InAppUpdateController into ChromeActivity coupled update logic to
base activity classes (Custom Tabs, WebAPKs) and required instanceof
casts in UpdateMenuItemHelper.

Move controller ownership to TabbedRootUiCoordinator:
- Lazily instantiate behind IN_APP_UPDATE_FLOW on first access.
- Wire lifecycle and activity results via ActivityLifecycleDispatcher.
- Gate resume checks on warm resumes via isWarmOnResume.
- Route menu clicks directly to UpdateMenuItemHelper, removing
  InAppUpdateManageable.
- Update unit tests in TabbedRootUiCoordinatorUnitTest,
  UpdateMenuItemHelperTest, and ChromeActivityUnitTest.

64ed75b2db7cc5c5f9bd622f9298d45dda001264

b5b7444a87546757602fe5c757e95b1832828814 [ diff ] 
[LHS] Smooth width transition when the rail expands/collapses

The NTP flexbox layout jumped to the new width whenever the LHS rail
changed size, while the rail itself animated.

Transition the width of cr-left-hand-side in both modes:

- Hybrid mode: the element owns the rail width and switches between the
  folded rail's width (64px) and the expanded width reported by the
  embedded page (261px by default), so the NTP slides smoothly between
  the folded and expanded states.

- Full-iframe mode: the embedded page owns the rail width and reports it
  through frame-sizing: content-width, which lands in one step. Observe
  the inner iframe with a ResizeObserver and mirror its width onto the
  host, which transitions it. The expanded state is inferred from the
  resize direction so the same expand/collapse timings apply.

Both modes read the width through `--cr-left-hand-side-width`, and the
transition is disabled when `--cr-animations-disabled` is set.

Bug: b:577172424

3fd400e4c38167d4a14a691dc1484263c3977e2b [ diff ] 
[CodeHealth][unsafe-buffers] Spanify data source IDs in ProducerHost::Flush

ProducerHost::Flush() built a vector from a raw pointer range using
UNSAFE_TODO pointer arithmetic. The perfetto::Producer interface forces
a pointer + size pair, so wrap it in a base::span once under a narrowly
scoped UNSAFE_BUFFERS with a SAFETY comment and copy it with
base::ToVector. Behavior is unchanged.

parent
8319410fa1769097d7aae4bb2d15b11c8b26cafc [ diff ] 
[buildbot] Allow remove_mixins to strip test_common mixins

In apply_common_transformations, test_common mixins (emitted from
Starlark test declarations) were applied with an empty ignore list
before remove_mixins was popped from the suite's test config. As a
result, a suite could not strip a mixin baked into an upstream test
declaration (such as skia_gold_test on chrome_public_test_apk) when
substituting a project-specific mixin.

Pop remove_mixins before expanding test_common and pass it to
apply_mixins. Existing generated JSON files are unchanged.

Revert "Increase the maximum file size for the large file experiment from 250MB to 2GB"

This reverts commit 3c1e56e2bf1e87440ec05b68c315998221b8ce74.

Reason for revert: Increasing default kMaxContentAnalysisFileSizeMB
from 250MB to 2048MB causes
ContentAnalysisDelegateBlockingSettingBrowserTest.BlockLargePages
(which uses a 251MB page) to no longer exceed the file size limit and
fail to trigger FILE_TOO_LARGE handling, deterministically timing out
on linux-chromeos-chrome.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8666488609671118625

9e8195a08cc85a3e3572d56c2901facce023cf36
parent
4f846ad38447437baee8b914f84f6ba4930c9064 [ diff ] 
Do not automatically save passwords if account changes

Previously, if a user signed out or switched accounts while resolving a
Trusted Vault error, the pending credential was automatically saved to
the local device store once the error cleared.

This CL ensures deferred auto-saving only proceeds if the primary
account matches the one from when error resolution started. Otherwise,
the deferred save is cancelled.

Demo of the behavior before introducing this fix:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjMzNDExNDU3MDQzNjYwOHwxNWYzNTE1ZC0wMw

Demo of the behavior with this fix:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTkwMTk5MDA1OTA0ODk2MHwyYTYwNjgwYS04NQ

[CodeHealth] Use log format specifiers in chrome/browser/ui/android/pdf

Convert string concatenation in Log.w/e calls in
chrome/browser/ui/android/pdf to format specifiers, for consistency
with docs/android_logging.md. Where an exception was concatenated into
the message (directly or via getMessage()), pass it as the trailing
Throwable argument instead so the stack trace is logged.

Also use pattern matching for instanceof in PdfCoordinator (same file
and owner), replacing instanceof checks followed by explicit casts.

No behavior change other than the added stack traces.

parent
89bd0df81010a98b593d3d0426c94cd3480e1993 [ diff ] 
[Sync] Wait for engine initialization before checking passphrase type

In SyncTest#testStopAndClear, clearServerData() triggers an asynchronous
sync engine reset, during which SyncServiceImpl.isEngineInitialized()
briefly returns false. Polling getPassphraseType() directly in
CriteriaHelper.pollUiThread trips assert isEngineInitialized() in
SyncServiceImpl.getPassphraseType(), failing the test with an unhandled
AssertionError.

Guard getPassphraseType() with isEngineInitialized() in the poll
predicate so CriteriaHelper continues polling until the engine finishes
re-initializing.

Validation: flake SyncTest#testStopAndClear failed 2/20 without this
change, 0/20 with it on
android_35_google_apis_tablet_x64_tablet_landscape_local

Fixed: 579818542

Media.EME.CdmStorageManager.CurrentDatabaseUsageKB.{ProfileType}

pattern matching in chrome/browser/password_manager/android, as
suggested by ErrorProne's PatternMatchingInstanceof check.

Mechanical change, no behavior change.

[webapps] Migrate banners, WebappsClient, and Android WebAPK [11/N]

Update WebAppBannerData, AppBannerManager, AppBannerManagerDesktop,
AppBannerManagerAndroid, WebappsClient, ShortcutInfo, WebappsUtils,
AddToHomescreenDataFetcher, PwaBottomSheetController,
WebApkUpdateDataFetcher, PwaInstallPageActionController, and
SamplingMetricsProvider to use blink::Manifest instead of
`blink::mojom::Manifest`.

Remove the temporary ManifestToMojom() shims in AppBannerManager,
AddToHomescreenDataFetcher, and WebApkUpdateDataFetcher, and place a
temporary ManifestToMojom() shim in
`WebappsClientDesktop::OnManifestSeen()` for VisitedManifestManager.

Reviewer Guide (Mechanical Migration):
- Boundary changes: Remove 3 temporary ManifestToMojom() shims from the
  parent CL (AppBannerManager, AddToHomescreenDataFetcher,
  WebApkUpdateDataFetcher) and add a 1-line ManifestToMojom() shim in
  WebappsClientDesktop::OnManifestSeen() (removed in the next CL).
- Mechanical changes (~95% of diff): Migrate WebAppBannerData,
  AppBannerManager, WebappsClient, ShortcutInfo, and unit/browser tests
  from blink::mojom::Manifest to blink::Manifest (`manifest.field` to
  `manifest.field()`, and Manifest::Builder in tests).

Bug: 485890278
Test: tools/autotest.py -C out/Default chrome/browser/banners/app_banner_manager_browsertest.cc
